			add_to_variable = { CAN_dnc_fortress_trade = -0.10 tooltip = CAN_dnc_fortress_trade_m10_tt }

			every_other_country = {
				limit = { is_major = yes }
				add_ai_strategy = { type = ignore id = CAN value = 50 }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = CANDNC_p_washington_will_answer_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				USA = { country_event = { id = can_dnc_r.468 days = 10 } }
			}
		}
	}
